This is not actionable, whatever its title says.\n   - **Wrong project:** the issue asks to change an *existing* function or\n     class by name, and `check_api.py` lists that name in `missing`. This may not work well if there is a long delay before you can start updating progress; you may need to wait for a response from a server or count files in a directory (for example). In these cases you can call :meth:`~rich.progress.Progress.add_task` with ``start=False`` or ``total=None`` which will display a pulsing animation that lets the user know something is working.
